## 12. Glossary

One meaning each; anything else gets another word. This glossary is[^dec-118]; the code renames it
implies (`StageState`, `StageProfile`, the Charts `ShotDiagnostics`, `review.excluded`) are[^dec-119].

| Id | Term | Meaning | Not to be confused with |
|---|---|---|---|
| GL-01 | Athlete | the aggregate root keyed by `AthleteId` | `RecordingHeader::Athlete`, a header struct |
| GL-02 | Athlete record | the athlete's source facts (OBJ-ATHLETE-RECORD) | the "athlete profile" (an old name for it) |
| GL-03 | Library | the root folder the repositories use; one per machine setting (SET-03) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-04 | Session | the aggregate root keyed by `SessionId`: one practice | a **peer session** (PPCP's), a **device session** (a launch monitor's files), `HmSessionWorker` |
| GL-05 | Session type | the kind of practice chosen at Start, kept for old files; it steers no analysis[^dec-202]; its steering is retired by #273 | `SessionProfile`, to be renamed `StageProfile` |
| GL-06 | Shot | the entity keyed by `ShotRef` | "swing", which is used only in file and folder names (`swing.ppsw`, `swing_NNNN`) |
| GL-07 | Swing number | the `NNNN` of `swing_NNNN`; "Shot N" | a rank "n of N" |
| GL-08 | Recording | the shot's captured source facts and its analysis context, written by capture | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-09 | Analysis context | the Recording's recorded block of athlete facts | the stage blackboard `AnalysisContext` (to be renamed `StageState`); the effective context |
| GL-10 | Effective context | the analysis context with the club correction applied, for one analysis run | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-11 | Recorded input | context frozen once for reproducibility | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-12 | Recorded product | an expensive derived fact cached with a stamp: only the shot's analysis | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-13 | Captured / device / declared club; club correction; effective club | §5.8 (the device club)[^dec-133] | the primary club (the record's default), follow-club (the device naming the club), `deviceClub` / `recordedClub` (code names) |
| GL-14 | Club set | men, women or junior; selects standard lengths | the norm cohort |
| GL-15 | Norm cohort | sex and age band; selects norms | the club set |
| GL-16 | Bag | the athlete's clubs and their geometry (OBJ-BAG); the *bag snapshot* is that geometry recorded in the Recording | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-17 | Reading exclusion | the board's "leave this reading out of the statistics" (`review.excluded` today) | trash |
| GL-18 | Trash | moving a shot or session to a recoverable place | exclusion |
| GL-19 | Archive | moving a session's streams to the archive mirror, leaving stubs | trash |
| GL-20 | Shot annotation | a golfer's judgement in the shot's `review` (an exclusion, a club correction) | **markup notes**, PPCP's annotations (FILE-SHOT-11) |
| GL-21 | Label correction | the golfer correcting a detected outcome label on one shot | a club's display label |
| GL-22 | Firing findings | the shot's assessment of its analysis and derived data against the diagnostic graph (GL-67)[^dec-132][^dec-140], each number against its corridor, with the context its session passes down, giving its *findings*[^dec-100][^dec-121] | "grading", today's word, no longer used at shot level |
| GL-23 | Finding | one result of firing, one per number checked; fired by the `Shot`[^dec-100], kept by its `Session`[^dec-135] | a "row" (today's code word); a table row |
| GL-24 | Reduction | a cross-shot rule run by a diagnostics owner | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-25 | Usuals | a session aggregate: the session's per-club typical values, computed from its shots' readings, never from findings; not part of the session's diagnosis; they flow down to the shots as an input to their derived data (M-SHOT-LAYERS layer 3)[^dec-130] | the diagnosis (GL-62) |
| GL-26 | Ledger | the session's diagnostic ledger: the `Session`'s collection of every shot's findings and derived data, kept in FILE-SESS-03 (`Diagnostics/derived/diagnostic_ledger.h`)[^dec-135] | the PPCP import ledger |
| GL-27 | Tiers | a condition's standing in a session's results (`Tier`: Clean · Watching · Pattern) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-28 | Links | the relations between conditions that the session reduction reports; its typed definition is step 3's (the session's result types) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-29 | Mixes | the session reduction's mix results; typed definition: step 3 (the session's result types) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-30 | Reasons | the reasons the session reduction gives for its outcomes (`SessionDiagnosticsModel::reasons` today); typed definition: step 3 | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-31 | Carry today | the session's carry per club against the usual | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-32 | Flight-check overlay | shot derived data: the flight check computed for the shot with the session's usuals passed down[^dec-122] | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-33 | The three factors, the shot's tendencies | strike · direction · flight: the shot's derived data[^dec-121] | **Tendencies** (GL-64), the athlete level's |
| GL-34 | Sky | the one flight fault: for a driver, fairway wood or hybrid it replaces High when the flight says so (`Diagnostics/derived/shot_factors.h`) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-35 | Screen answers | the golfer's answers to a session's screens (the screen pack, `screens.json`) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-36 | Intent run | a "from here on" intent, pre-filling each later shot | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-37 | Work-on | Coaching's (§2): a priority (GL-71) that the golfer or the coach adopts, with its lifecycle (its states are designed in the coaching track; `docs/design/coaching_layer.md` proposes them). The declared work-on (GL-68) is one[^ctx-24]. The golfer keeps the word; until the coaching track builds adoption, the panel that shows a session's priorities says "Priorities", not "Work-ons"[^dl-priorities] | a priority (GL-71), the session's ranked, classed fault, which is Diagnostics' |
| GL-38 | Held condition | a condition's "held" state, decided by one rule that Your swing and Work-ons share (R-DER-7) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-39 | Session summary | what the athlete level reads from a session (FILE-SESS-05) | `SessionSummary` (a display struct); `swing_summary.json` (a shot cache); the analysis' `summary` block |
| GL-40 | Club baseline | a session's frozen per-club baselines (OBJ-CLUB-BASELINE) | the parity baseline (a test fixture); the ball's empty-mat baseline |
| GL-41 | Catch-up | the application service that brings the athlete level's caches up to date | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-42 | Words module | the one place golfer-facing sentences are built | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-43 | Context | one of the four contexts (§2): Acquisition, Diagnostics, Coaching, Domain + Application. They are contexts, not layers: "layer" means the shot's layers (§2.4, M-SHOT-LAYERS) or P2's (GL-45)[^ctx-24] | the analysis context (GL-09); `ShotContext`; QML context globals; a layer (GL-45; M-SHOT-LAYERS); a ring (GL-44) |
| GL-44 | Ring | a dependency ring (§4) | a layer |
| GL-45 | Layer | one of P2's three: domain, application state, view state | a ring |
| GL-46 | Stage | an analysis stage (`k*StageVersion`) | Stage 0 (the refactor's first phase, named only); the UI's *stage panel* |
| GL-47 | Profile | n/a: avoid; say athlete record, studio profile, stage profile or capture profile | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-48 | Rig | Studio/Rig (OBJ-RIG) | the skeleton `Rig` (`Skeleton3d`), `SwingRigDriver` |
| GL-49 | The lead | the agent that leads the refactor; it wrote this page's first version | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-50 | Live | (1) of a session: in progress; (2) of a path: capture, as against re-analysis (the live join, the live job); (3) of an object: the in-memory one other code may be changing (P8's "live object"). Say "real-time" for real time | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-51 | Corridor | a norm's range for one number, against which a finding is fired | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-52 | Condition | a named pattern a pack defines (a fault or a set-up), which findings and the session's diagnosis report | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-53 | Pack | a set of conditions and the rules that relate them (the core pack, the user's pack, community packs) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-54 | Norm set | a file of corridors (the core norms, the user's corridor edits, community sets) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-55 | Content set | the user's drills, references, gate words or screens (FILE-APP-03) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-56 | Theme | an athlete-level swing theme from the themes reduction (FILE-ATH-03) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-57 | Gate | a check that decides whether a number may be trusted (`gate_record`, the gate catalogues) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-58 | Route | the instrument path that produced a measurement (part of its source) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-59 | Stub | an archived shot's swing file, thinned to its summary, rows and review (FILE-SHOT-10) | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-60 | Window time | time relative to the start of the shot's analysed window (`analysis.timeBase: "window"`) | the capture clock |
| GL-61 | Measurement family | the catalogue `group` of a metric (`MetricDescriptor::group`, e.g. "Wrist & forearm"); a metric added within an existing group is not an IMC (§1.2)[^dec-107] | n/a: no other meaning |
| GL-62 | Diagnose, diagnosis | the session's work over its shots' findings: tiers, patterns, links, reasons, per-club mixes, the summary; reserved for the session[^dec-100]. The usuals are not part of it[^dec-130] | firing findings (the shot's) |
| GL-63 | Shot derived data | layer 3 of M-SHOT-LAYERS: the shot outcome, the shot's tendencies (its three factors), carry against the usual and the flight-check overlay, computed for the shot with the session's usuals passed down; they feed the assessment and are not findings[^dec-121] | findings (GL-23) |
| GL-64 | Tendencies | the athlete level's tendencies, from session summaries (FILE-ATH-02) | the shot's tendencies (GL-33) |
| GL-65 | Assessment | layer 4 of M-SHOT-LAYERS: the shot's analysis and derived data checked against the diagnostic graph (GL-67, the diagnostic engine)[^dec-132][^dec-140], which fires its findings[^dec-121] | the session's diagnosis (GL-62); 03's older cross-shot "assessment", now "diagnosis"[^dec-125] |
| GL-66 | Analysis graph | the per-shot graph of versioned analysis stages that makes the shot's analysis ([§2.1](#21-the-shapes-of-diagnostics))[^dec-132] | the diagnostic graph (GL-67); bare "DAG", retired in this page |
| GL-67 | Diagnostic graph | the diagnostic engine's condition graph (pack + norms), which a shot's analysis and derived data are assessed against to fire its findings[^dec-132] | the analysis graph (GL-66); bare "DAG", retired in this page |
| GL-68 | Declared work-on | the condition the golfer declares they are working on: the declared focus, part of the session intent (OBJ-SESSION-INTENT)[^dec-138]; a work-on (GL-37), and Coaching's starting point (§2)[^ctx-24] | the session priorities (GL-69); the athlete priorities (GL-70) |
| GL-69 | Session priorities (the session work-ons before v1.10.0) | the session summary's ranked list of priorities (GL-71; OBJ-SESSION-SUMMARY)[^dec-138][^ctx-24] | the declared work-on (GL-68); the athlete priorities (GL-70); a work-on (GL-37) |
| GL-70 | Athlete priorities (the athlete work-ons before v1.10.0) | the athlete level's priorities (GL-71), kept by `AthleteDiagnostics`[^dec-138][^ctx-24] | the declared work-on (GL-68); the session priorities (GL-69); a work-on (GL-37) |
| GL-71 | Priority | Diagnostics' ranked, classed fault: what the session's diagnosis computes today (`session_reduction`), at the session (GL-69) and athlete (GL-70) levels. Its types are renamed at Stage B, Part 1 of the platform refactor (for example `WorkOnClass` → `PriorityClass`) and move to `src/Diagnostics/derived/` with today's `src/Coaching` files; `work_ons.json` (FILE-SESS-05) keeps its name[^ctx-24]. The panel that shows them says "Priorities"[^dl-priorities] | a work-on (GL-37): a priority the golfer or the coach adopts, which is Coaching's |

## 13. Decisions register

Every decision this page relies on. The ids are stable; 03's decision numbers are kept as DEC-01…DEC-30, so "03
decision 24" and DEC-24 are the same decision. 03 holds the rationale for DEC-01…DEC-30.

| Id | Decision | Source |
|---|---|---|
| DEC-01 | Every first-class domain object is C++; QML holds no domain objects or rules | 03 decision 1 |
| DEC-02 | Always grade: every live shot is graded whether or not a panel is on | 03 decision 2; under DEC-100: every live shot fires its findings |
| DEC-03 | Golfer input is stored at its level | 03 decision 3 |
| DEC-04 | The athlete library folder is stored on the record, kept on rename, migrated once | 03 decision 4; "stored on the record" reworded by DEC-151 (the folder is where `athlete.json` is found) |
| DEC-05 | `athlete.json` in the library; settings keep only "current athlete" | 03 decision 5 |
| DEC-06 | The flight check is a session-level overlay on unchanged rows | 03 decision 6; superseded by DEC-122 (shot derived data) |
| DEC-07 | Direction: the pack's corridors everywhere | 03 decision 7 |
| DEC-08 | The score pill: a design proposal for later | 03 decision 8 |
| DEC-09 | Held conditions: one rule | 03 decision 9 |
| DEC-10 | The new layer is `src/DomainModel` | 03 decision 10 |
| DEC-11 | A separate `AppState` in `src/App`; sessions have a status; repositories are identity maps | 03 decision 11 |
| DEC-12 | All domain changes go through aggregate-root commands, with domain events | 03 decision 12; "aggregate-root commands" reworded by DEC-104 (an entity's own facts by its own commands, reached through its root) |
| DEC-13 | Shot label corrections and shot intent on the shot's `review` block | 03 decision 13 |
| DEC-14 | The strangler migration of 04, the lint first | 03 decision 14 |
| DEC-15 | Instruments: the LM a device; one source-tagged measurement set; one precedence rule | 03 decision 15 |
| DEC-16 | The Recording contract | 03 decision 16 |
| DEC-17 | Studio/Rig: named now, designed in the platform refactor (capture) | 03 decision 17 |
| DEC-18 | A corrected club: re-analysis uses the effective club | 03 decision 18 |
| DEC-19 | Grading by `SessionDiagnostics`; the pipeline never grades | 03 decision 19; "`SessionDiagnostics` grades every shot" superseded by DEC-100 (the `Shot` fires its own findings; the session diagnoses) |
| DEC-20 | Intent runs pre-fill later shots; an edit affects only later shots | 03 decision 20 |
| DEC-21 | Recorded input, a third kind in P1 | 03 decision 21 (its rewrite rule refined by DEC-46) |
| DEC-22 | The five events; the cast with nothing captured; every file through its root, the archive and the report as `Session` commands, a swing file once by capture then by its `Session` | 03 decision 22 (its tuning-log writer superseded by DEC-43); "then by its `Session`" superseded by DEC-86 (then only by the `Shot`); `SessionRelocated` removed by DEC-154 |
| DEC-23 | The Platform ring | 03 decision 23 |
| DEC-24 | IM-1: the `analysisContext` block | 03 decision 24; step 1's brief §8.2 |
| DEC-25 | IM-2: the input stamp and the time base | 03 decision 25 |
| DEC-26 | IM-3: the club rule until step 6 (the effective club field by field: R-CLUB-3) | 03 decision 26 |
| DEC-27 | IM-4: capture writes in stages | 03 decision 27 |
| DEC-28 | IM-5: the interim writers until step 3 | 03 decision 28; "the `Session` takes them over" superseded by DEC-86 (the `Shot`) |
| DEC-29 | IM-6: `src/Recording` and `src/DomainModel` in step 1 | 03 decision 29 |
| DEC-30 | IM-7: no norm cohort in step 1 | 03 decision 30 |
| DEC-31 | Step 1's D1: the golfer's correction is the LM board's override only, until step 6 | step 1's brief §9 (Solution Architect, 14 Nov 2026) |
| DEC-32 | Step 1's D2: a corrected club's length from a snapshot of the bag's geometry in the Recording | as DEC-31 |
| DEC-33 | Step 1's D3: one schema-tagged `analysisContext` block; the cohort recorded is the club set | as DEC-31 |
| DEC-34 | Step 1's D4: backfill height and club set into the old library once, dry run to the Solution Architect first, backups in the shared scratch area | as DEC-31 |

| DEC-39 | Step 1's D9: after a club correction, stale only | as DEC-31 |
| DEC-40 | This page is canonical; 03 is the frozen rationale | Solution Architect, 14 Nov 2026 (information-model audit, D1) |
| DEC-41 | `SessionId` is a uuid minted at Start, stored in a session file in the folder with the status and start/end times; the folder is a property only | as DEC-40 (D2) |
| DEC-42 | P3: each file has exactly one writer, inside its aggregate; derived owners write their own caches; source facts change only through the root's commands | as DEC-40 (D3); "the root's commands" reworded by DEC-104 |

[^built-6-3b]: Built in refactor step 6.3b (`build/refactor/step-6/BRIEF-6.3b.md`), from R-DER-6 and the audit's F-05 / G-01, G-02 and G-04 (`docs/implementation/swe-audit/02-findings.md`); the duties inventory is `build/refactor/step-6/DUTIES-6.3b.md`. Its judgement calls and proposals (`build/refactor/step-6/NOTES-6.3b.md`) are for the Solution Architect and the Coaching Lead to confirm. The athlete's handedness as the fallback for a shot that records none is the coordinator's call J-101 (its fix round, the same NOTES); recording handedness in device-only recordings is proposed there for the Solution Architect.
[^j106-legacy-stub]: INTERIM, pending the Solution Architect: one read-time exception for legacy `DRIVER` stubs on the launch monitor board. A legacy stub is a shot whose `review.club` reads `DRIVER`, with no captured club and no device club, and which is not a device-only shot (`swingDocIsLegacyClubStub`, beside the one club rule in `src/Vocabulary/Club/club_vocabulary.h`). Before step 6.2, capture wrote that `DRIVER` when the session had no club. With no correction, the board counts such a shot under the club its readings specifically point at (source `evidence`), as it did before 6.2. The note offers Undo, which stores the recorded club as the golfer's correction. Every other reader, and every other shot, follows the one club rule, and the exception writes nothing. The flag is derived when the summary is read. It is never stored in the document; a JSON-era swing's summary sidecar caches it. New captures never write a stub. One case cannot be told apart from a stub: a shot captured since 6.2 in a session with no club, on which the golfer then picks Driver on the popover. The proposed ruling would read a legacy stub as undeclared everywhere, and then this exception goes. Coordinator's call J-106 (`build/refactor/step-3/JUDGEMENT-CALLS.md`), built in step 6.2's fix round (`build/refactor/step-6/NOTES-6.2.md`).

[^built-6-2]: Built in refactor step 6.2 (`build/refactor/step-6/BRIEF-6.2.md`), from R-CLUB-1 to R-CLUB-5, DEC-133 and DEC-67, and the audit's G-07 and B-07 (`docs/implementation/swe-audit/02-findings.md`); the duties inventory is `build/refactor/step-6/DUTIES-6.2.md`. It covers review finding B1 and the implementer's earlier call to keep records and sensors outside P2's list. Built by C-5b's fix round.
[^sa27-p]: Solution Architect, 27 Nov 2026, attended questions (the rulings comment on #377): the Rig's four preconditions for the cameras' cut-over, each as recommended: P1, the camera keys gone from settings leave the Rig as it is; P2, entries this build cannot read kept as read and written back, outside the state and its digest; P3, an unreadable `rig.json` renamed `rig.json.unreadable-<UTC time>` and kept, a newer schema's file read-only as before; P4, a failed save remembered and tried again at the next command and at quit, with no notice the golfer sees. Built by platform refactor C-5b.

[^sa-staging]: Solution Architect, 23 Nov 2026: the print renders into the cache staging folder and the `Session` moves the PDF into the session, so the `Session` is the PDF's one writer.
[^sa-shot-changed]: Solution Architect, 23 Nov 2026.

[^sa-r-ins-5]: Solution Architect, 23 Nov 2026: ranking applies to metrics a device reports; a metric no device reports is estimated from all the evidence, not picked from one input. The low point (no supported launch monitor reports it) is the first case. Its estimator and an audit of every derived metric against R-INS-5 follow the refactor (GitHub #229 and #230).
[^sa-vocab-root]: Solution Architect, 23 Nov 2026: one root folder for the Vocabulary ring, areas inside it; the remaining areas move in the platform refactor (mandatory).

Buffer's part was withdrawn on 25 Nov 2026 (`[^sa25-buffer]`) and reinstated the same night (`[^sa25-buffer-back]`): its stream types move in Stage D.
[^sa24-q11]: The Solution Architect, 24 Nov 2026 (`docs/implementation/platform_refactor_assessment.md`, "Rulings of 24 Nov": Q11, C9): the studio profile goes to `src/App/`, beside `AppState`, in the Application ring where its owner is. It keeps its own file (P11); Stage E gives it its repository. Built in platform refactor B1.9.
[^sa25-saving]: The Solution Architect, 25 Nov 2026 (`build/platform/A/NOTES-280.md` §11): `shotSaving` is one read-only fact, computed by the shot processor (the service capturing and saving the shot) and shown on `app.state`; the dialogs wait on it and call End themselves.

[^sa25-buffer-back]: The Product Owner and the Solution Architect, 25 Nov 2026 (later the same night as `[^sa25-buffer]`): lever D-V3 reversed. Buffer's stream types move to `src/Vocabulary/` in Stage D as J-109 ruled (`[^sa24-q15]`), keeping the Solution Architect's mandate of 23 Nov 2026 that the architecture lint ends with one Vocabulary rule (`[^sa-vocab-root]`). `docs/implementation/platform_d_capture_seam.md`, "The lever pass of 25 Nov". The wording of this change was approved by the Solution Architect, 26 Nov 2026 (`build/platform/JUDGEMENT-CALLS.md`, "Morning batch 1", ruling 6).
[^sa26-qml-check]: The Product Owner and the Solution Architect, 26 Nov 2026: the QML-rule check lands with every screen file that holds rules frozen at its count on a shrink-only exception list, each line pointing to an issue, grouped by area; the check stays where it is built, `session_setup_lint_test` W12, and is R-QML-4's check; the list's owner is the Solution Architect; R-QML-4's exceptions are the calibration screens (#291), the Consistency grade words (#219, the Design Lead's) and the frozen files, and AD-52 (`governance/controls/build.md`) matches. The Solution Architect approved the wording on 26 Nov 2026.
[^sa26-battery]: The Solution Architect, 26 Nov 2026 (the midday rulings, item 2): the battery rule goes in `src/Vocabulary/Rig/battery_band.h`, a pure function (a level and whether the device is charging in; ok, low or very low out); the sensor manager, the phone host and the Resource Monitor publish the band beside the level, and QML only maps the band to a theme token; the information model gets one row: the band is derived, never stored. The bands and their colours are the Product Owner's and the Design Lead's, 26 Nov 2026 (one set for every device).
[^sa25-add2]: The Solution Architect, 25 Nov 2026: the Rig design addendum's eleven decisions, ruled as recommended (`docs/design/rig_design_addendum.md`); decision 2: a calibration record's `evidence` pointer is outside the rig state's digest, like `sinceUtc`, and ID-RIG's row gains those words (C5).
[^sa25-add1]: The Solution Architect, 25 Nov 2026: the Rig design addendum's eleven decisions, ruled as recommended (`docs/design/rig_design_addendum.md`); decision 1: the managers ask the Rig to act through `RigCommands`, interfaces among the Domain model's contracts (`rig_commands.h`) that `Rig` implements and the composition root hands to each; the header joins lint A9's named contract headers.
[^sa25-add8]: The same ruling; decision 8: a manager is handed both parts for its own devices as typed values and is told of every change to either; a change to an alias or an enabled flag emits a second event, `RigDeviceLabelChanged`, after the save, carrying the device key and which of the two changed; it recomputes nothing and makes no new rig state; a learned prior emits nothing.
[^sa25-add9]: The same ruling; decision 9: two sensor keys are equal when their device ids are, as built (C-1a); the alias map is looked up by its own spelling, never by key equality. Built in C-1c and C-3; its sentence written into ID-DEVICE-KEY by C-5a.2.
[^sa25-add10]: The same ruling; decision 10: a camera key with no `|` is nothing (the type gets no rule for it); the copy from settings keeps what it cannot type, verbatim, and matches it to no device. Built by C-5a.2, which keeps such entries in the store, beside the two parts (a coordinator's call, CHECK 3.3), and covers them in the group's fingerprint (CHECK 3.5).

An information-model change (C7, C8). None of it is built.
